# 计算机使用智能体

计算机使用智能体是完整的系统：观察屏幕或网页，制定计划，再通过点击、输入、浏览器命令或命令行工具完成用户任务。

> AwesomeCUA 目录收录 481 条已审核智能体。每条附有完整注释、一手证据和许可证信息。快照：2026-11-10T06:29:47Z。

HTML 页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/categories/agent/ · [English](https://awesomecua.com/categories/agent/index.md) · 目录索引：https://awesomecua.com/llms.txt · API：https://awesomecua.com/api/v1/resources.json

## 常见问题

### 目录中被提及最多的智能体有哪些？

[Hermes Agent](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-nousresearch-hermes-agent/)（28 条记录）、[Mobile-Agent](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-x-plug-mobileagent/)（27 条记录）和 [OpenClaw](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-openclaw-openclaw/)（17 条记录）在其他目录记录的已审核来源中被提及最多。

### 这些智能体覆盖哪些平台？

在 481 条已审核智能体中，网页 247 条、桌面 229 条、手机 95 条、跨平台 60 条；3 条未记录平台。标注多个平台的条目在每个平台下各计一次。

### 哪些智能体有对应的论文？

481 条中有 70 条链接到提出或说明它的 arXiv 论文，例如 [Mobile-Agent](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-x-plug-mobileagent/)、[Agent S](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-simular-ai-agent-s/) 和 [SeeAct](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-osu-nlp-group-seeact/)。

### 智能体使用哪些许可证？

最常见的声明许可证是 MIT（210 条）、Apache-2.0（90 条）和 AGPL-3.0（13 条）。481 条中有 146 条未记录声明的许可证。

### 这个列表按什么排序？

按在已审核来源中提及该条目的目录记录数排序，多者在前，数量相同时按名称排序。列表上方的排序菜单可切换为 A–Z。

## Jev Ultrafast

## OpenMausBot

桌面聊天应用，其机器人可运行本地 Claude、Codex 或 Grok CLI，并各自通过 Cua Driver 使用云端 Linux 桌面、本地虚拟机、VPS 或宿主机，还内置浏览器和审批卡片。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-milind-soni-openmausbot/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/milind-soni/OpenMausBot
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、macOS、Linux、网页
- 主题：沙箱、人机协作、工具集成
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T11:40:28Z
- ID：github:milind-soni/openmausbot

### 范围记录

OpenMausBot ships no model: an Electron shell talks to a local harness server that runs the claude, codex and grok CLIs or other ACP engines and normalises their events. Host control uses the bundled Cua Driver and needs explicit opt-in; the README marks it supported on macOS, beta on Ubuntu Xorg and disabled on Wayland. Cloud computers come from the third-party Boat service and connected apps from Composio. The README says the project was formerly OpenGrokBot. Everything outside enterprise/ is Apache-2.0; enterprise/ is source-available under its own license.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-milind-soni-openmausbot/record.json

## VibeSurf

带 Chrome 扩展界面的浏览器助手，可跨标签页并行运行 Browser Use 智能体，通过 Langflow 拖放式浏览器工作流执行登录、数据收集和研究任务，使用本地或托管 LLM。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-vibesurf-ai-vibesurf/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/vibesurf-ai/VibeSurf
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、工作流
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-19T06:20:04Z
- ID：github:vibesurf-ai/vibesurf

### 范围记录

pyproject.toml pins browser-use 0.9.5; the README also credits LangGraph and Langflow, and the tree vendors a langflow directory. It installs as a uv tool, a Windows installer or a Docker image with noVNC; from Chrome 142 the extension must be loaded manually. Integrations include Composio and site APIs for Xiaohongshu, Douyin, Weibo and YouTube. The LICENSE is a 'VibeSurf Open Source License' that adds multi-tenant, SaaS, frontend-logo and contributor conditions to Apache-2.0, while pyproject.toml declares plain Apache-2.0, so the code license is left unknown. Latest release 0.5.18 (2026-01-17).

## Gemini Browser Agent

Chrome 扩展和 Python WebSocket 桥接器，在用户当前标签页运行 Gemini 2.5 Computer Use，将标签页截图发给模型，并通过内容脚本执行点击、输入和滚动。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-pmbstyle-gemini-browser-agent/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/pmbstyle/gemini-browser-agent
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、自动化
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T08:41:39Z
- ID：github:pmbstyle/gemini-browser-agent

### 范围记录

The README calls the project a research experiment and scaffolding. websocket_agent.py is a local WebSocket server that calls gemini-2.5-computer-use-preview-10-2025 through google-genai with the browser computer-use environment; widget/ is an unpacked Manifest V3 extension with a side panel where the user connects to the bridge and enters a goal. Each turn the extension captures the visible tab with chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab, the bridge converts the model's 0-1000 normalized coordinates to viewport pixels, and the extension carries out clicks, typing, key presses, hovering, scrolling and navigation by injecting functions (chrome.scripting.executeScript) that dispatch synthetic DOM events at document.elementFromPoint. Drag and drop is approximated with mousedown and mouseup events, unhandled model actions return an unimplemented warning, and the side panel sets a step limit (default 10). It needs a Gemini API key and acts in the user's own browser without a sandbox; the README points to the author's separate gemini-computer-use repository for sandboxed runs. The repository has no license file at the inspected commit.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-pmbstyle-gemini-browser-agent/record.json

## jev-use

macOS 语音和文字计算机使用应用，遍历前台应用的无障碍树，由托管的 Jev 模型逐步选择操作和编号目标，再通过无障碍操作及模拟输入执行。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-savka777-jev-use/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/savka777/jev-use
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、macOS
- 主题：自动化、效率、人机协作
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T07:55:46Z
- ID：github:savka777/jev-use

### 范围记录

The Swift app (built as Desktop Voice.app; macOS 14.2+ and Xcode) reads the front window's Accessibility tree, skipping secure text fields, and sends the command, app and window names, numbered targets and recent actions to TypeSafe's hosted systemone endpoint with model jev-latest; no screenshots are sent (Sources/JevDesktop/Desktop.swift, Sources/JevCore/Decision.swift). Actions use Accessibility presses and value setting plus CGEvent mouse, key and scroll events. An optional OpenRouter key enables a planner model (default inception/mercury-2.5) that splits a spoken command into steps; Jev still chooses each on-screen target (Sources/JevCore/Planner.swift). Input is Apple Speech hold-to-talk, hands-free mode, an optional "Hey Jev" wake phrase, or typed commands. The README says low-confidence and destructive picks stop and ask. Jev is a hosted model.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-savka777-jev-use/record.json

## OpenAI Computer Use Sample Apps

OpenAI 参考智能体通过 Playwright JavaScript 操作浏览器，或通过 PyAutoGUI Python 操作桌面，由模型经 Responses API 编写代码，并附有模拟看板、绘图和预订实验环境。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-openai-openai-cua-sample-app/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/openai/openai-cua-sample-app
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页、桌面
- 主题：工具集成、网页导航、可观测性
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T11:14:13Z
- ID：github:openai/openai-cua-sample-app

### 范围记录

The repository (ID 946708780, created 2025-03-11) keeps its original name and a description about using CUA through the API, but the inspected README and python-app/app/responses_loop.py now drive the model through persistent code-execution function tools (exec_py for PyAutoGUI; a JavaScript worker for Playwright) rather than the computer-use tool, so no relation to the OpenAI CUA model record is asserted. The default model is configurable. Each run copies a lab template and records screenshots and a replay trace. The README states that generated code runs with user permissions, without an operating-system sandbox or OpenAI's production action-review controls, and that a final answer does not prove task success. MIT applies to the sample code.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-openai-openai-cua-sample-app/record.json

## Orca

Rust 编写的 DeepSeek 原生终端编程智能体，内置 macOS 计算机使用驱动；经逐应用批准后，可查看其他应用的无障碍树和截图，并点击、输入、发送按键和滚动。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-echovic-orca-agent/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/echoVic/orca-agent
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：相关研究
- 运行环境：桌面、macOS
- 主题：工具集成、安全与防护
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T08:18:08Z
- ID：github:echovic/orca-agent

### 范围记录

Published by echoVic as the npm package @blade-ai/orca and native binaries for macOS, Linux and Windows; it is primarily a coding agent that reads and edits code and runs commands with a DeepSeek API key. Computer use is one feature: on macOS 14 or later, in the main interactive TUI session, a `computer` tool backed by the bundled crates/orca-computer-use driver lists and launches apps, observes a window as an accessibility-element outline with ids plus an optional screenshot, and clicks, types, sends keys and scrolls on targets (protocol.rs, observation.rs). Per the computer-use docs, each app needs approval unless a rule or full-auto mode allows it, typing into secure password fields always asks separately, password managers and finance apps are blocked by default, Orca never operates its own host terminal, plan mode only lists and observes, and Esc stops the run. Browser tools used from the separate Pilion Browser come from that host. Root LICENSE is MIT; the latest GitHub release is v0.5.12.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-echovic-orca-agent/record.json

The repository is the scaffold for Convergence AI's proxy-lite-3b model: a CLI (proxy), a Streamlit app and a Runner that loops a solver and a webbrowser environment. By default it calls a demo endpoint on Hugging Face Spaces, which the README reserves for demonstrations; it recommends serving convergence-ai/proxy-lite-3b with vLLM and the hermes tool-call parser for production. The webbrowser environment (src/proxy_lite/environments/webbrowser.py) launches Chromium through Playwright with playwright_stealth, injects JavaScript to find interactable elements, and by default sends a screenshot annotated with numbered bounding boxes plus a text list of the marked elements; earlier screenshots are dropped from the context. BrowserTool exposes goto, google_search, click, type, scroll, back, wait, reload and do_nothing actions addressed by mark_id. The README says the model is meant to complete tasks autonomously and may struggle with logins or user-confirmed steps. Model weights are not in this repository. Code was read.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-convergence-ai-proxy-lite/record.json

## Roubao

Kotlin 编写的设备端 Android 手机智能体，无需 PC 即可通过 Shizuku 截图、点击、滑动和输入，使用任意兼容 OpenAI 的 VLM、GUI-Owl 或 MAI-UI 运行规划、执行和反思循环。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-turbo1123-roubao/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/Turbo1123/roubao
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：Android
- 主题：手机操作、自动化、规划、自我反思
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T07:57:16Z
- ID：github:turbo1123/roubao

### 范围记录

Kotlin Android app (minimum SDK 26) published by Turbo1123. Shizuku gives it ADB-level shell access without root, which DeviceController.kt uses for screencap, input tap/swipe/text and am start; the agent's floating overlay is hidden while each plain screenshot is taken. In the default mode MobileAgent.kt runs Manager (plan), Executor (one of click, double_tap, long_press, type, swipe, system_button, open_app, wait, take_over or answer, with pixel coordinates), ActionReflector (compares the before and after screenshots) and an optional Notetaker over an OpenAI-compatible VLM API, for at most 25 steps by default; the README calls this layer a Kotlin port of Mobile-Agent-v3. Two other modes call GUI-Owl through Alibaba Cloud DashScope's GUI agent endpoint (default model pre-gui_owl_7b), which returns actions directly, or an OpenAI-compatible MAI-UI server (default localhost:8000, MAI-UI-2B) with MAI-UI's prompt format and up to three history screenshots. A Skills layer (app/src/main/assets/skills.json) can instead hand a request to another app through a deep link, and Tools cover app search, deep links, clipboard, shell and HTTP. When screencap is blocked on a secure screen the agent asks the user before continuing; the Manager prompt stops the run when a screen actively asks for payment confirmation, a password or biometric verification, and the Executor can hand logins and captchas to the user (take_over) or ask for confirmation of payment actions. The README says a 2.0 version based on AccessibilityService is developed on a separate branch; this record describes main. Root LICENSE is MIT; latest release V1.4.2.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-turbo1123-roubao/record.json

## Webagent (webagent.cloud)

可自行托管的浏览器智能体服务器，运行 Browser Use 或 Notte 智能体，将运行记录中的操作转为参数化工作流，再通过 Playwright 重放，步骤失败时交由智能体处理。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-webagent-cloud-webagent/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/webagent-cloud/webagent
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：自动化、工作流、网页导航
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T07:41:55Z
- ID：github:webagent-cloud/webagent

### 范围记录

Open-source code of the webagent.cloud hosted service, published by webagent-cloud. A FastAPI server (server.py, webagent/api.py) with a bundled React UI accepts a prompt and runs it with the engine set by ENGINE_PROVIDER: Browser Use by default, or Notte, whose agent is fixed to gemini-2.5-flash with at most 30 steps. The browser comes from BROWSER_PROVIDER: a local Playwright Chromium over CDP, Browserbase or Steel; at the reviewed commit the Steel provider's create_session takes a context argument that the caller omits. Models are reached through Browser Use's chat clients for OpenAI, DeepSeek, Anthropic, Google and Groq; other provider values fall back to the OpenAI client. After a run, workflow_builder_service.py asks an LLM to extract parameters from the prompt and template the recorded actions with their element XPaths; workflow_replay_service.py replays those steps with Playwright (navigation, search, XPath clicks and typing, scrolling, key presses, uploads, dropdowns) without a model and, when an action fails, hands that step to the AI engine for up to three attempts. Tasks, runs and results are kept in a local SQLite database; results can be text or JSON validated against a supplied JSON Schema, and a webhook can be called on completion. The README's n8n, Zapier and Make integrations and Stagehand engine are not in the tree. Root LICENSE is MIT.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-webagent-cloud-webagent/record.json

## AI Browser (DeepFundAI)

Electron 桌面浏览器，内置从基于 Eko 的 npm 包导入的智能体，可规划可编辑的多步工作流并操作自身标签页中的网页，同时提供聊天、定时任务和记忆功能。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-deepfundai-ai-browser/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/DeepFundAI/ai-browser
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、工作流、人机协作
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T15:41:31Z
- ID：github:deepfundai/ai-browser

### 范围记录

The repository holds the Next.js and Electron app: tabs as Electron WebContentsView instances (electron/main/services/tab-manager.ts), scheduling, memory, settings, and the agent wiring in electron/main/services/eko-service.ts. The BrowserAgent and FileAgent themselves are imported from the npm packages @jarvis-agent/core and @jarvis-agent/electron (0.3.x), which the README describes as based on Eko; their npm metadata names FellouAI as author and a placeholder repository URL, and their source is not in this tree. In Deep Explore mode the user confirms or edits the generated workflow, and the agent can ask questions during a run. The README calls it a learning project developed and tested mainly with DeepSeek models. Releases ship macOS and Windows installers.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-deepfundai-ai-browser/record.json

## AI Mime

macOS 应用录制演示任务，由智能体将其编译为包含脚本、浏览器 CDP 步骤和计算机使用回退机制的可移植技能，并在重跑失败时修补该技能。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-prakhar1114-ai-mime/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/prakhar1114/ai_mime
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：macOS、桌面、网页
- 主题：录制、工作流、自动化、鲁棒性
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T08:40:23Z
- ID：github:prakhar1114/ai_mime

### 范围记录

Requires macOS 13 or newer, Python 3.12 and Accessibility, Screen Recording and Input Monitoring permissions; Windows support remains pending. The recorder (pynput listeners and mss screenshots) writes a manifest of clicks, typing, hotkeys and scrolls with pre-action screenshots, plus optional extract and detail notes. Reflection turns the trace into a coordinate-free schema.json; an optimizer writes optimized_plan.json, preferring script steps, then browser_harness steps (Chrome CDP through a browser-harness git submodule), then ui_agent steps; a build agent packages a Claude Skill-compatible folder with run.sh, inputs and a fallback plan. The ui_agent drives the Mac in the foreground through cua-computer-server MCP tools, which AI Mime extends with combined screenshot-plus-accessibility-tree reads, and is told to target accessibility elements before screenshot coordinates. Agent runtimes are Anthropic (Claude Code) or OpenAI (Codex), selected during onboarding, with custom providers in user_config.yml. A prebuilt macOS DMG is linked from the README.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-prakhar1114-ai-mime/record.json

## AI SDK Computer Use

Vercel Labs 的 Next.js 聊天应用，让 Claude 的计算机和 bash 工具在 Vercel Sandbox 中操作 Linux 桌面，通过 xdotool 点击和输入，并经 noVNC 将屏幕实时传至页面。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-vercel-labs-ai-sdk-computer-use/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/vercel-labs/ai-sdk-computer-use
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、Linux
- 主题：沙箱、工具集成
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T04:02:46Z
- ID：github:vercel-labs/ai-sdk-computer-use

### 范围记录

Published by the vercel-labs GitHub organization. At commit ed103da the chat route calls claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929 through the AI SDK with Anthropic's computer_20250124 and bash_20250124 tools. lib/sandbox/tool.ts executes clicks, drags, typing, key presses and scrolling with xdotool on display :99 at 1024x768 and captures plain full-screen screenshots with ImageMagick import. lib/sandbox/create-snapshot.ts installs Xvnc, openbox, Google Chrome, noVNC and websockify and builds xdotool from source. The Vercel template page vercel.com/templates/next.js/ai-sdk-computer-use republishes the README. No code was run.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-vercel-labs-ai-sdk-computer-use/record.json

单个 APK 中运行的设备端 Android 智能体，通过无障碍服务操作任意应用，读取元素概要，必要时读取带标签的 8×12 网格截图，并使用任意兼容 OpenAI 的模型。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-kuafuai-andee/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/kuafuai/andee
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：Android
- 主题：手机操作、GUI 定位、记忆、安全与防护
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T08:00:27Z
- ID：github:kuafuai/andee

### 范围记录

Kotlin Android app for Android 11 (API 30) or later, published by KUAFUAI, with a floating-ball persona, voice input and output, an encrypted on-device notebook and scheduled scenes. One AccessibilityService is the entry point. ScreenController.kt renders the pruned accessibility tree as an outline with one line per element, an element id, a clickable mark and a centre in 0-1000 relative coordinates; for apps that draw their own pixels the model gets a screenshot (accessibility takeScreenshot, or MediaProjection per ScreenCapture.kt) overlaid with an 8-column by 12-row labelled grid, taps by cell and which third of it, and can request a zoomed view with a red crosshair before committing. A per-location guard refuses a fourth tap within 120 px of the same spot inside five minutes. Text goes in through the accessibility input channel on Android 13 or later, with ADBKeyboard as the fallback. LocalBrain.kt runs a non-streaming tool-calling loop against any OpenAI-compatible endpoint for a configurable number of steps (1 to 200); the brain can instead run elsewhere and reach the device over a WebSocket hub. The README states that the dispatcher stops before a purchase's final confirmation, never enters payment passwords, and types vault passwords without returning them to the model. Optional extras include a robot dog driven through a USB OTG dongle. Root LICENSE is the Andee Open Source License: Apache-2.0 text with additional conditions (no white-labelling, no multi-tenant hosting, written authorisation to ship it inside another product); GitHub reports NOASSERTION. The README says ML Kit barcode and play-services stubs bundled with the app are not open source. Release v0.5.2 tags the inspected commit.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-kuafuai-andee/record.json

## Bah

Electron AI 浏览器，其侧边栏智能体读取 DOM 或 Accessibility 树中带编号的页面元素、页面文字和 OCR 结果，再通过 Chromium 输入事件点击和输入，可使用云端或本地 Ollama 模型。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-alexvilelabah-bah-browser/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/alexvilelabah/bah-browser
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、人机协作、自动化
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T15:46:09Z
- ID：github:alexvilelabah/bah-browser

### 范围记录

Browser application by VilelaLab with Windows and macOS builds (release v1.9.2). The agent loop observes each page through an injected script or the CDP accessibility tree (src/renderer/page-executor.ts), adds tesseract.js OCR text, and attaches a page screenshot only when the configured model accepts images (src/main/ai-engine.ts); clicks and keys go through Electron sendInputEvent on the page (src/main/main.ts). Tools include element-reference clicks and fills, coordinate clicks, tabs and navigation, plus non-interface skills such as image generation, video and price lookups. The README states a 25-step cap, confirmation before paying, buying, deleting or entering card data, and a hand-off on CAPTCHAs and login walls. It runs in the user's persistent logged-in session, presents a Chrome user agent and masks navigator.webdriver, and the app also bundles an ad blocker, password manager, media downloader and torrent client. Supported providers are DeepSeek, Mistral, NVIDIA NIM and Ollama.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-alexvilelabah-bah-browser/record.json

## Bops

面向业务运营机器人团队的 Mac 应用，在 Orgo 云端 Linux 桌面上通过 OpenAI 计算机工具工作，执行 xdotool 操作，并结合 Playwright 页面脚本、应用连接器、批准机制和实时接管。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-orgoai-bops/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/OrgoAI/bops
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、Linux、macOS、网页
- 主题：自动化、工作流、人机协作、工具集成、网页导航
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T08:17:25Z
- ID：github:orgoai/bops

### 范围记录

Electron app (desktop/) that starts a local Next.js server (app/, lib/server/) on port 3210; version 0.0.28 in package.json, matching release tag v0.0.28 at 92ec73d, and the inspected commit 62c7f98 is later. Bots' tasks run on Orgo cloud computers launched from the bops-base template that orgo/bops-base.mjs builds from vm/ (Codex, the screen MCP and per-screen Chrome helpers). By default lib/server/computer-task.ts runs each thread on OpenAI's Responses API computer tool: Orgo's API returns a screenshot of the bot's screen, and every click, double-click, drag, scroll, typing or key press the model asks for is executed as xdotool commands through Orgo's bash endpoint, with the result screen sent back; the same loop offers a page_script tool that runs a short Playwright script on the screen's Chrome over CDP, a Linux shell, web search, a password vault and app, data and CRM tools. With BOPS_COMPUTER_TOOL=0 threads use OpenAI's Agents API with Codex and vm/screen_mcp.py, which exposes one screen's screenshot and input actions over MCP. Tasks on the user's own Mac run in a bot-owned Chrome; with Full access they also get a shell and the user's apps through Cua Driver behind vm/mac-ui-mcp.mjs, which hides sensitive apps, sends input only in the background and blocks quitting, copying and pasting (lib/server/local.ts). Per the README, reading runs at once while sending, creating and paying wait for the user's approval, and any screen can be watched live and taken over. Self-hosting requires an Orgo account and two OpenAI API keys, with Honcho, Composio, treg, AgentMail, AgentPhone and TypeSafe recommended or optional; signing in with Orgo instead routes provider calls through Orgo's hosted Bops Cloud (cloud/). Licensed FSL-1.1-ALv2 (fair source; competing products and hosted services are excluded, and each version becomes Apache-2.0 two years after release); components/message-ui/ is adapted from MIT-licensed code.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-orgoai-bops/record.json

## BrowseGPT

Chrome 扩展配合本地 Node.js 服务器，将可见页面的链接、按钮和输入框列为编号大纲，并向 OpenAI gpt-3.5-turbo 询问下一步 CLICK、ENTER_TEXT 或 NAVIGATE 操作以完成目标。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-danielpquinn-browsegpt/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/danielpquinn/browsegpt
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、自动化
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-19T07:16:33Z
- ID：github:danielpquinn/browsegpt

### 范围记录

Inspected at commit c86c148e (23 July 2024), the latest commit on the default branch. The content script assigns IDs to visible, in-viewport elements and sends the first 100 outline lines with the goal, URL and up to five previous steps to the server, whose few-shot prompt asks gpt-3.5-turbo for one JSON command with a reason; the extension then calls click(), enters text or changes the location. Each submission runs one step, and elements already acted on are left out of later outlines. The Manifest V3 extension is labelled version 2.0. The browsegpt.ai site and its llms.txt describe Claude Haiku 4.5 as the default model with OpenAI GPT as an option; the public repository's server calls only the OpenAI API. The Chrome Web Store build remains uninspected. The repository has no license file, so the code license is unknown. No code was run.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-danielpquinn-browsegpt/record.json

## Browser Agent

Python 智能体通过 CDP 将 Playwright 连接到本地 Chrome，把 DOM 中可见交互元素映射为编号 ID，再由 LLM 浏览、点击、输入、选择并向用户询问缺失信息。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-rkvalandas-browser-agent/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/rkvalandas/browser_agent
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、自动化、人机协作
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T16:15:38Z
- ID：github:rkvalandas/browser_agent

### 范围记录

Single-author personal project. browser/browser_setup.py connects to Chrome on localhost:9222 through connect_over_cdp and launches Chrome with remote debugging when none is running. browser/analyzers/page_analyzer.py runs a DOM script that classifies elements by tag and ARIA role, builds unique selectors and detects modal overlays; no screenshots are taken. docs/BROWSER_TOOLS.md documents the tool set (analyze_page, navigate, go_back, scroll, click, type, select_option, keyboard_action, ask_user). LLM backends are OpenAI, Azure OpenAI, Groq or Anthropic (agent/llm_providers.py). No evaluation results are published.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-rkvalandas-browser-agent/record.json

## Browser Agent (uiuing)

Chrome 侧边栏智能体的页面引擎读取整页语义 DOM 快照，通过元素指纹重新定位目标，派发 DOM 事件，并在报告成功前检查操作后的条件。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-uiuing-browser-agent/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/uiuing/browser-agent
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、状态追踪、工作流
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T15:56:55Z
- ID：github:uiuing/browser-agent

### 范围记录

Chrome Manifest V3 extension by uiuing, first released as v0.0.1 (Chrome zip in GitHub releases). The model acts on pages only through the page_act tool, which hands a goal to an engine that builds a semantic snapshot of DOM nodes with roles, accessible names, values, occlusion, open shadow roots and same-origin iframes (packages/extension/src/engine/page/perception.ts), resolves targets by fingerprint against a fresh snapshot and fires click, input, change and key events in the page (engine/page/execute.ts). The page_screenshot tool shows a capture to the user; its description limits the model to other page information rather than the pixels (tools/page.ts). A chrome.debugger channel is used for file uploads and falls back to DOM synthesis. Tools carry read, act or dangerous risk tiers with site allow/block lists and confirmation; verified runs can be saved as skills and replayed over batch rows. Model ports cover OpenAI-compatible, OpenAI Responses and Anthropic APIs. The docs/benchmark.md comparison is publisher-run on the project's own fixtures against its own replica of a viewport-only baseline.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-uiuing-browser-agent/record.json

The repository is a modified Chrome DevTools frontend; the AI Chat panel holds the agent framework, accessibility-tree and extraction tools, memory, MCP client and evaluation code, and agent-server is a Node.js HTTP and WebSocket wrapper that sends tasks to agents and captures pages over CDP. The LICENSE is the Chromium Authors' BSD-3-Clause text inherited from DevTools; third_party components keep their own terms.

The README offers a macOS Apple Silicon DMG, a Windows installer and a Linux AppImage, with deb and rpm packages on the releases page. ## Buddy

Android 应用通过无障碍服务提供本地 MCP 手机工具服务器，使设备端 proot Linux 中运行的 Claude Code、Codex 或 OpenCode 能读取屏幕、点击、输入和打开应用。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-ghorbelhamdi-buddy-android/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/ghorbelhamdi/buddy-android
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：Android
- 主题：手机操作、人机协作、安全与防护、工具集成
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T08:13:05Z
- ID：github:ghorbelhamdi/buddy-android

### 范围记录

Requires a 64-bit Android 11+ phone; the README says it was tested on one OnePlus 12 and uses no adb, PC or root. Tools.java registers the phone tools; read_screen walks the accessibility node tree of the visible windows into a numbered list, and tap acts on those nodes or their clickable ancestors, with a separate screenshot tool. The agents (Claude Code, Codex or OpenCode) are installed into an Alpine Linux that Buddy runs under proot and use the user's own plan or OpenCode's free models; no model is bundled. Actions that send, post, buy, delete or change settings go through confirm cards, and per-app Trust, Hard or Smart rules are enforced by the app; the README names prompt injection from on-screen text as the main risk. benchmark/ reports a small timing comparison of seven model and agent pairs on five phone tasks, two runs each, checked by hand on one phone.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-ghorbelhamdi-buddy-android/record.json

- 代码：Apache-2.0 — https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ghorbelhamdi/buddy-android/HEAD/LICENSE
- 数据：未记录
- 文档：未记录

## Cetus

用于运行和审阅编码智能体运行环境的 macOS 客户端；内置运行环境含通过带编号的 Accessibility 元素操作前台应用的计算机使用辅助工具，并支持 Chrome 控制。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-drewnekota-cetus/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/drewnekota/cetus
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：相关研究
- 运行环境：桌面、macOS、网页
- 主题：自动化、人机协作
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T15:39:54Z
- ID：github:drewnekota/cetus

### 范围记录

Adjacent: Cetus is mainly a review board and launcher for Claude Code, Codex, DeepSeek Harness, OpenCode, Grok Build, Kimi CLI and its own runtime (a bundled pi agent), with scheduled runs, per-run git worktrees and on-device screen-history OCR and meeting transcription. GUI control lives in two opt-in plugins of the built-in runtime. The computer-use plugin tunnels requests through the Rust host to a long-lived Swift helper (src-tauri/cua/cetus-cua-helper.swift) that dumps the frontmost or named app's Accessibility tree as numbered elements; computer_act presses or sets values by index and falls back to synthetic CGEvent clicks and keystrokes, asking for confirmation before the first consequential action and before destructive or sensitive ones, and computer_verify re-observes. The browser-use plugin attaches the external chrome-devtools-mcp to Chrome on port 9222. Apple Silicon only, macOS 13+ (Tauri build) or 15+ (Swift beta). Root LICENSE is MIT.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-drewnekota-cetus/record.json

## ClosePaw

本地运行的 Android 智能体应用读取无障碍树，可选用截图，通过无障碍节点操作或手势点击执行任务，也可在后台的 Shizuku 虚拟显示屏上运行。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-imoonkey-closepaw/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/imoonkey/closepaw
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：Android
- 主题：手机操作、人机协作、记忆
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-19T07:04:42Z
- ID：github:imoonkey/closepaw

### 范围记录

Kotlin app for Android 12 or later with an on-device ReAct loop, subagent delegation, todo and scratchpad tools, markdown memory files and per-app skills. Perception defaults to the accessibility tree; screenshot-only and hybrid modes are options (perception/PerceptionConfig.kt). Clicks try an accessibility ACTION_CLICK on the resolved node and fall back to a dispatched gesture tap (tool/action/ClickExecutor.kt, platform/NodeActionPerformer.kt, platform/AccessibilityGestureInjector.kt). Optional power tools use Shizuku for a background virtual display, Termux for a Linux shell, and Chrome DevTools Protocol scripting of Chrome. A bundled tier list blocks banking, payment, crypto-wallet, password-manager and authenticator packages (assets/security/app_tiers.json), and other apps need per-app approval. Models are reached through OpenAI-compatible endpoints or a ChatGPT/Codex sign-in. The eval/ harness drives AndroidWorld task setup and scoring and launches the app over ADB.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-imoonkey-closepaw/record.json

## CUGA

企业通用智能体框架通过 API、MCP 或 OpenAPI 工具规划任务，在网页或混合模式下经浏览器扩展操作页面，并提供意图约束、工具审批和工具约束。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-cuga-project-cuga-agent/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/cuga-project/cuga-agent
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、工具集成、产业、安全与防护
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T11:58:18Z
- ID：github:cuga-project/cuga-agent

### 范围记录

The README calls it a Configurable Generalist Agent built with IBM; IBM's papers expand the name as Computer Using Generalist Agent. The default task mode is API-only; web and hybrid modes run the browser agent (planner, action agent with click, type, select, go-back and scroll tools, and page understanding) through a browser extension. NOTICE lists files derived from BrowserGym. The README cites arXiv 2510.23856 for its WebArena result; that paper is not in the catalog, and the curated arxiv:2503.01861 describes the CUGA system without naming this repository, so no relation is recorded.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-cuga-project-cuga-agent/record.json

DomA 浏览器智能体 Chrome 和 Edge 扩展的公开源码版：通过带标记的截图和元素列表观察网页，以 DOM 事件和 CDP 点击操作，并经 MCP 提供工具。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-dom-actions-doma/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/dom-actions/doma
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、自动化、工具集成
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T15:43:31Z
- ID：github:dom-actions/doma

### 范围记录

The README presents DomA as a browser agent from Stay Browser, and the LICENSE notice names Dom Actions; the commercial store build adds video download, a userscript manager and ad blocking on top of this core. The service worker implements browser_* tools (tabs, navigation, page content, element lists, click, text click, type, select, scroll, script execution, clipboard). browser_screenshot tags up to 150 visible interactive elements with data-som-idx and composites numbers and borders onto a clean viewport capture, grouping the overflow into areas A1 to A5; clicks fall back to CDP Input.dispatchMouseEvent through chrome.debugger. With the Jev switch on, a high-confidence Jev decision can execute a click or type without sending the image to the LLM. Models are user-supplied OpenAI-compatible endpoints. An MCP bridge (stdio server plus a local daemon on ports 3846/3847) and a CLI let Cursor, Claude or OpenClaw drive the extension.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-dom-actions-doma/record.json

- 数据：未记录
- 文档：未记录
- 权重：未记录

## DSHBox

Android 应用，在 PRoot Debian 沙箱中本地运行 DeepSeek Harness 智能体，并为其提供 WebView 浏览器 CLI，以及通过 Accessibility 和 Shizuku 后端操作原生应用的手机助手。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-wsk-build-dshbox/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/WSK-build/DSHBox
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：相关研究
- 运行环境：Android、网页
- 主题：手机操作、人机协作、工具集成
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T15:45:48Z
- ID：github:wsk-build/dshbox

### 范围记录

Adjacent: the app's main job is hosting DeepSeek Harness (DSH), a coding-agent runtime, on Android 10+ ARM64 without root or Termux, alongside a terminal and file manager; the GUI executors are bundled tools that DSH chooses between. The browser workspace exposes 24 tools through a bundled browser-use plugin and a sandbox browser CLI (observe, locate, click on screenshot coordinates, type, scroll, screenshot, plus opt-in CDP debugging). Pilot, the phone assistant, lists 39 capabilities and reaches the app layer through a file-based mailbox; its vendored interlock-relay core has an Accessibility backend (node actions and gestures on the default display), a Shizuku shell backend that can also act on a background virtual display, and direct platform calls, behind approval gates. The README declares GPL v3 for the project; pilot/NOTICE marks interlock-relay-core as Apache-2.0 and the DSHBox adapter as GPL-3.0, and PRoot, Debian and other components keep their own terms. README.md is in Chinese and README.en.md in English.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-wsk-build-dshbox/record.json

## Eido

基于 Zed 和 pi agent 的 macOS 编程工作空间，新增实验性浏览器操作能力：内置智能体选取编号页面元素并通过 CDP 操作；计算机使用则通过内置 Cua Driver MCP 服务器实现。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tony3liu-eido/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/tony3liu/eido
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：相关研究
- 运行环境：桌面、macOS、网页
- 主题：网页导航、工具集成、人机协作
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T08:34:39Z
- ID：github:tony3liu/eido

### 范围记录

Coding is the primary purpose: the README frames release v0.0.1 (Apple Silicon, macOS 13 or later, locally signed and not notarized) around a describe, investigate, edit, review and verify loop, and calls browser and desktop automation experimental. server.mjs runs an eido-browser MCP server with browser_* tools. Its default Ultrafast engine, adapted in TypeScript from browser-use/jev-ultrafast, launches Playwright Chromium, injects snapshot.js to list up to 250 visible interactive elements with roles and labels plus visible page text, has a decision model choose one click, fill, select, scroll or wait step at a time, and dispatches mouse and key events over the DevTools protocol (browser.ts); per server.mjs, screenshots serve recording and the inspector, with model input drawn from the page listing. config.mjs points decision requests at the hosted TypeSafe API (model jev-latest) by default and, unless overridden, generates typed text with the user's main model. A Legacy engine uses the jev-browser npm dependency. computer.mjs registers the bundled or a user-installed cua-driver as the eido_computer MCP server, which needs macOS Accessibility and Screen Recording permission; native/cua/upstream.json pins trycua/cua tag cua-driver-rs-v0.34.0, and per NOTICE the native/cua/overlay patches add session-owned background focus, exact-window keyboard guards and window capture validation. NOTICE licenses Eido-original code GPL-3.0-or-later, while Zed components marked Apache-2.0, the Cua Driver and jev-ultrafast-derived code (MIT), pi (MIT) and upstream pi-acp adapter code (Apache-2.0) keep their own terms.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-tony3liu-eido/record.json

## EverFern

Electron 桌面智能体通过 LangGraph 编排器运行截图驱动的计算机使用循环，以 robotjs 输入鼠标和键盘，并结合 Navis 浏览器智能体与 shell 工具，支持本地或云端模型。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-everfern-ai-everfern/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/Everfern-AI/Everfern
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、Windows、macOS、Linux、网页
- 主题：自动化、网页导航、工具集成、规划
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T08:15:50Z
- ID：github:everfern-ai/everfern

### 范围记录

package.json at the inspected commit names the app desktop, version 1.3.0, MIT. computer-use.ts captures the screen through Electron, downscales screenshots to 1280 px wide, asks a configurable vision model for the next action (its class defaults name bytedance/ui-tars-1.5-7b as the action model), maps the returned normalized coordinates to the screen and executes mouse and keyboard input with @jitsi/robotjs. The Navis browser agent has an extension mode, whose companion extension is published in a separate repository, and a Playwright mode (browser-control-adapter.ts). The README lists Ollama, LM Studio and cloud providers, and describes runtime tool synthesis behind human approval, saved skills, peer-agent debate and shell commands run in an isolated Linux VM.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-everfern-ai-everfern/record.json

## Fan

Electron 桌面智能体通过编号 DOM 和无障碍快照操作内嵌的可见 Chromium 浏览器，运行有边界的 JavaScript 浏览器事务，并在登录和高风险步骤交还控制权。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-7757-fan-browser-agent/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/7757/Fan-Browser-Agent
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、人机协作、自动化
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T16:07:48Z
- ID：github:7757/fan-browser-agent

### 范围记录

Early-preview desktop app (v0.4.3 unsigned builds for macOS, Windows and Linux were the latest GitHub release at review). The README states that its Python agent runtime is adapted from Nous Research's Hermes Agent under MIT, and the root LICENSE keeps both copyright notices. Observation (apps/desktop/electron/browser-runtime/observation/operations.cjs) combines CDP DOMSnapshot.captureSnapshot with Accessibility.getFullAXTree into a numbered snapshot; browser_snapshot can attach a plain screenshot or one with numbered element boxes. browser_run executes model-written JavaScript against a sandboxed fan.* API, clicks go through Input.dispatchMouseEvent, and browser_handoff returns control to the user. The runtime also has terminal, file, memory, MCP and scheduled-task tools. Models come from user-configured providers.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-7757-fan-browser-agent/record.json

- ID：github:agent-labs-dev/fastbrowse

### 范围记录

Python package fastbrowse 0.5.23 by Agent Labs (Python 3.13+), with a CLI, an MCP server (fastbrowse-mcp) and a JavaScript SDK. The browser layer speaks CDP through cdp-use; snapshot.js, ported from jev-ultrafast, indexes each frame's controls, viewport text and a freshness fingerprint, including open shadow roots. Per the design document, Jev chooses the operation and target among indexed controls (with relevance pruning on dense pages), an LLM proposes start addresses, plans checkable requirements, reads page text, writes non-secret field text and composes the answer, and code enforces freshness and hit-tests before input, no automatic retry of mutations, authorization for submit, pay, delete or send, secret redaction and budgets; a run counts as complete only when every requirement is evidenced. Browsers are Browser Use Cloud by default, local Chrome, or an existing CDP endpoint including Electron windows. Jev is reached through TypeSafe, OpenRouter or the Vercel AI Gateway. Benchmark comparisons in the README are the publisher's. Root LICENSE is MIT, with a NOTICE file.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-agent-labs-dev-fastbrowse/record.json

## Index

已归档的 Laminar Python 浏览器智能体，使用具视觉能力的 LLM 操作 Playwright Chromium，返回 Pydantic 结构化结果，并提供可选 Laminar 追踪的交互式 CLI。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-lmnr-ai-index/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/lmnr-ai/index
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、可观测性、人机协作、历史
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T11:37:39Z
- ID：github:lmnr-ai/index

### 范围记录

GitHub reports the repository as archived, with the last push on 2025-06-09. The tree holds the agent, Playwright browser and controller modules (actions include give_human_control and spreadsheet-cell clicks), providers for Anthropic, Bedrock, Gemini, Vertex, OpenAI and Groq, and two structured-output eval scripts. The README also advertises a hosted serverless Index API and chat UI run by Laminar. Apache-2.0 covers repository code only.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-lmnr-ai-index/record.json

### 范围记录

Community Manifest V3 extension that its README describes as a port of browser-use/jev-ultrafast and as unaffiliated with TypeSafe or Browser Use. The content script (src/content/snapshot.ts) lists visible, hit-tested interactive elements with role, accessible name, value and link target, plus up to 6,000 characters of page text; no screenshots are sent. Jev is a hosted model reached through OpenRouter, TypeSafe or Cloudflare Workers AI; TYPE_TEXT values come from an OpenAI-compatible text model. Clicks and keystrokes are dispatched through chrome.debugger (src/background/input.ts), with synthetic DOM events when that is switched off. The README reports 13 of 17 publisher-chosen tasks passing in one recorded headless-Chromium run (docs/e2e-suite-2026-09-19.md), an internal test suite.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-chy4pro-jev-for-chrome/record.json

## Login Machine

面向网站登录的浏览器智能体循环：Claude 根据截图和精简 HTML 判断登录界面，并返回经 DOM 验证的 Playwright 定位器；用户输入的凭据不发送给模型。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-richardhruby-login-machine/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/RichardHruby/login-machine
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、人机协作、安全与防护
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T12:26:18Z
- ID：github:richardhruby/login-machine

### 范围记录

Built by @RichardHruby and @jesse-olympus at Anon as a Next.js app. Each step sends a JPEG screenshot and form-relevant HTML (walking into shadow roots) to claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929, which returns one of six typed screen states (credential_login_form, choice_screen, magic_login_link, loading_screen, blocked_screen, logged_in_screen) with Playwright locators; locators unresolved in the live DOM are fed back for up to three analysis attempts. Browsers are Browserbase sessions reached over CDP, so Anthropic and Browserbase keys are required. The user supplies credentials, choices and magic links through the chat UI, and Playwright types or clicks them. package.json is a private app (login-machine 0.1.0); a hosted demo is linked from the README.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-richardhruby-login-machine/record.json

## LongHorizonUI

LongHorizonUI Android 智能体代码：OCR 与 OmniParser 图标检测生成带编号的元素框供 LLM 使用；点击失败时从元素索引回退到相对或绝对坐标，并支持回滚。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-kane2kang-longhorizonui/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/kane2kang/LongHorizonUI
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：Android
- 主题：长任务、手机操作、界面理解、自我反思
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T07:39:57Z
- ID：github:kane2kang/longhorizonui

### 范围记录

Code release of the ICLR 2026 paper "LongHorizonUI: A Unified Framework for Robust long-horizon Task Automation of GUI Agent" (the repository description says ICLR2026; the README cites it with anonymous authors and no link). Each screenshot passes through RapidOCR, the OmniParser v2 YOLO icon detector (downloaded from microsoft/OmniParser-v2.0 on first use) and template matching when an icon template directory is set; the merged elements are drawn as numbered boxes, and that annotated screenshot with a step prompt is what the Gemini, Azure OpenAI or OpenAI model receives. Actions are click, long press, swipe, drag, text input and key press. With the fallback cascade on (the default), an element-index target that fails is retried as a relative point inside the box and then as absolute coordinates with jitter; a progress monitor can roll back to the last committed snapshot, at most three times per task. The README documents only the offline mode, which replays a task's recorded screenshot sequence with either the task description or step-by-step instructions. run.py also has a live subcommand that lists USB-connected devices, but at the reviewed commit AndroidContext never opens a device connection (its context stays None), so device actions return without acting. The repository also has a Gradio app, agents that turn recorded taps and swipes into step descriptions, a scrcpy-based Android recording tool, step-level evaluation scripts and the project page source. The LongGUIBench dataset is not released (the README link is a placeholder), the requirements.txt the README installs from is not in the tree, and the README's Apache-2.0 badge and LICENSE link are not backed by a license file. Not a fork, not archived; no release.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-kane2kang-longhorizonui/record.json

## MacOS Agent (BIGAI)

macOS 智能体使用 smolagents 代码智能体调度各应用子智能体；子智能体读取无障碍树和窗口截图，通过 pyautogui 鼠标与键盘输入执行操作。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-computer-use-agents-macos-agent/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/Computer-use-agents/MacOS-Agent
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、macOS
- 主题：跨应用、规划、自动化
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-19T06:14:40Z
- ID：github:computer-use-agents/macos-agent

### 范围记录

Released by the Computer-use-agents organization; the project page credits the BIGAI ML Group. macosagent/macosagent.py builds a smolagents CodeAgent whose tools are ten app agents (macosagent/agents/__init__.py): Browser, Calendar, Excel, Finder, Player, PowerPoint, Preview, TextEdit, WeChat and Word. The browser agent wraps the external browser-use library (agents/browser_agent/browser.py). In the Calendar agent, the window is captured with screencapture, interactive elements from the macOS accessibility API are drawn as numbered boxes on that screenshot, and the image plus element list go to the model (calendar/calendar.py, calendar/context.py, calendar/utils.py, agent/service.py); clicks and typing use pyautogui and pynput (controller/service.py). The Excel agent likewise clicks and types with pyautogui and runs AppleScript to save and close workbooks (excel_agent/controller/service.py). The README's License section names CC BY-NC 4.0, but the inspected tree has no license file.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-computer-use-agents-macos-agent/record.json

This entry covers the published engine; Mobster for Mac is a separate product. The documented reference example requires an already-serving WebDriverAgent runner for an iPhone and a model-service key; it performs a fixed read-only Settings task and declines approvals. The repository lists benchmark/evaluation tooling, but dated simulator subsets, real-device trials and earlier engine results are different evaluation populations. The README explicitly places several product modules outside this repository. Source inspection confirms WDA dispatch paths. MIT applies to the inspected repository software, not automatically to external WDA code, images or other data.

## Open ChatGPT Atlas

Chrome 侧边栏扩展和 Electron 浏览器，使用 Gemini 2.5 Computer Use 读取标签页截图并生成点击、输入、滚动和导航操作，另提供可选的 Composio 工具路由模式。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-composio-community-open-chatgpt-atlas/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/composio-community/open-chatgpt-atlas
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、工具集成
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T12:20:03Z
- ID：github:composio-community/open-chatgpt-atlas

### 范围记录

The repository moved from ComposioHQ/open-chatgpt-atlas to composio-community (same GitHub repository ID 1080954405); the README star badge still links the former name. In Browser Tools mode the extension captures the visible tab (chrome.tabs.captureVisibleTab), sends it to gemini-2.5-computer-use-preview-10-2025 and executes the returned actions by dispatching synthetic DOM mouse and keyboard events at the given coordinates from its content script; the Electron app captures its view with webContents.capturePage and injects equivalent JavaScript. Model calls go directly from the client with a user-supplied Google API key; the Composio key for Tool Router mode is optional. There is no LICENSE file and GitHub reports no license, so the code license is unknown. No GitHub releases; the extension manifest is version 1.0.0.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-composio-community-open-chatgpt-atlas/record.json

## OpenBrowser (OpenBrowserAI)

Chromium 补丁集内置助手扩展，浏览器智能体查看索引化页面元素和带编号框的截图，再按元素编号点击、输入、滚动和切换标签页。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-openbrowserai-openbrowser/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/OpenBrowserAI/openbrowser
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、规划、工具集成
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T16:11:41Z
- ID：github:openbrowserai/openbrowser

### 范围记录

Three parts: chromium/ holds a patch list and scripts that rebrand a separately checked-out Chromium source tree with the root branding_assets/ and load the assistant as a built-in component extension in a side panel (no prebuilt browser is released); chromium-extension/ is that assistant (sidebar chat, options, background agent); packages/core is the TypeScript agent library with planning, replanning, a chat agent, MCP clients, memory compression and web search. Its browser agent injects a DOM-tree script that indexes clickable elements as pseudo-HTML and, in the default normal mode with draw marking, sends a viewport capture with the indexed boxes drawn on it; tools navigate, click, type, scroll, hover, select options, extract page content and switch tabs, and the extension executes them through chrome.tabs, chrome.scripting and chrome.debugger. Models are configured with the user's own API keys. Root LICENSE is MIT.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-openbrowserai-openbrowser/record.json

## OpenBUA

在用户已登录的浏览器中运行的 Chrome 侧边栏智能体，读取 DOM 控件清单和标签页截图，再通过注入的合成事件点击、输入和填写表单。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-alisharjeell-openbua/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/AliSharjeell/OpenBUA
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、记忆、自动化
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-19T06:11:56Z
- ID：github:alisharjeell/openbua

### 范围记录

Chrome Manifest V3 extension (manifest version 1.9.1) whose agent loop is built on the pi-agent-core and pi-ai packages. Its tools (src/agent/tools.ts) read a paginated inventory of interactive DOM controls including open shadow roots, inspect forms, click elements or screenshot-pixel coordinates (used for canvas editors such as Google Docs), type, scroll, switch and open tabs, upload files, post to social sites and send webmail; capture_tab_screenshot returns the visible-tab image to the model. Actions run as scripts injected with chrome.scripting that dispatch synthetic pointer and mouse events (src/agent/browser-bridge.ts); the manifest requests no debugger permission. Before showing its human CAPTCHA gate, the bridge makes up to two automatic rounds in which a vision model is asked to click inside detected challenge regions (src/agent/captcha-auto.ts). It acts in the user's logged-in sessions and keeps a memory store with optional parallel worker windows. The README ends with 'MIT', but the inspected tree has no license file.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-alisharjeell-openbua/record.json

## OpenComet

Chrome 侧栏智能体为页面元素添加编号框，在模型查看截图和 DOM 文本前于设备端遮盖人脸、凭据字段及文本个人信息，再通过选择器或调试器点击执行操作。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-princechouhan19-opencometai/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/princechouhan19/OpenCometAI
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、安全与防护、界面理解
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T16:19:20Z
- ID：github:princechouhan19/opencometai

### 范围记录

The README presents it as a Smart India Hackathon entry for problem statement 26171, whose listed organisation is ISRO. The Manifest V3 extension (version 1.31.0 at review) is loaded unpacked in Chrome; the README says Firefox has been built from the same source since v1.17.0. src/content/dom-detector.js paints numbered boxes on interactive elements, and the service worker captures the tab through the Chrome debugger or captureVisibleTab, so screenshots carry those marks. In privacy mode, MediaPipe, YOLO, ViT, Tesseract OCR and regex/checksum PII detectors run in the browser, and src/lib/privacy-firewall.js blocks or blanks a frame when sanitizing fails. Actions use selectors, with debugger Input.dispatchMouseEvent clicks where needed, inside a tab-group sandbox with an approval mode. Models: on-device Gemma 4, cloud providers, or Ollama through the bundled server. OpenCometBench is the repository's own test suite; its PII precision/recall uses a synthetic corpus built from the detector's own rules, as the README states, leaving independence outside the measurement.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-princechouhan19-opencometai/record.json

## OpenPhone (Secondly)

基于 LineageOS 的 AI 原生 Android 系统；其特权助手通过新增框架服务读取屏幕和 UI 树，跨应用点击、滑动和输入，并提供确认、监视器和审计日志。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-secondly-com-openphone/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/secondly-com/OpenPhone
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：Android
- 主题：手机操作、人机协作、安全与防护
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T15:46:31Z
- ID：github:secondly-com/openphone

### 范围记录

Developer preview based on LineageOS 23.2 / Android 16, targeting the Pixel 9a first, with an emulator build path. The repository holds an overlay (the privileged OpenPhoneAssistant app and vendor configuration), patch stacks for LineageOS repositories and build scripts. The frameworks_base patches add an agent manager service with task-scoped input action execution, a framework-mediated UI tree snapshot, opt-in screenshots captured by system_server, a pending-action confirmation flow and a tamper-evident audit log. overlay/vendor/openphone/config/openphone_model_tools.json lists 54 model-visible tools, including screen reading, tap, swipe, typing, app launch, messages, calendar, watchers and background jobs. Models are reached through a development model broker in services/. It is unrelated to the HKUDS OpenPhone mobile foundation-model paper (arXiv 2510.22009).

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-secondly-com-openphone/record.json

## Opus

macOS Electron 应用，其 OpenAI Agents SDK 循环依据元素列表和截图，通过 AppleScript、无障碍元素点击、按键、shell 命令及 URI 操作应用。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-jeffrey-zang-opus/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/jeffrey-zang/opus
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、macOS
- 主题：自动化、工具集成、界面理解
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T08:11:19Z
- ID：github:jeffrey-zang/opus

### 范围记录

initAgents.ts defines an app-selection agent and an action agent, both configured with gpt-4.1-mini through the OpenAI Agents SDK; the name is unrelated to Anthropic's Claude Opus models, and an OpenAI API key is required. Each action step receives the target app's AppleScript dictionary, a list of clickable Accessibility elements (role, title, value, help and description) and, when captured, a screenshot (runAgents.ts). Swift helpers under app/swift read the Accessibility tree, click, send keys and move the app window to a separate virtual display so the agent can work in the background. The repository also contains the tryop.us landing site and a small server.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-jeffrey-zang-opus/record.json

## OS AI Computer Use

本地桌面智能体，将纯截图发送给 OpenAI 或 Anthropic 的计算机使用工具，并通过基于 PyAutoGUI 的驱动在 macOS、Windows 和 Linux 上执行返回的点击、拖拽、滚动和按键操作。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-777genius-os-ai-computer-use/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/777genius/os-ai-computer-use
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、macOS、Windows、Linux
- 主题：自动化、跨应用
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T15:38:22Z
- ID：github:777genius/os-ai-computer-use

### 范围记录

Provider adapters call the OpenAI Responses API with its computer tool (batched actions) or Anthropic's computer-use tool (single actions with zoom); packages/core/src/os_ai_core/tools/computer.py captures the screen and executes actions through per-OS driver ports. macOS uses PyAutoGUI with a Quartz event path for Enter and an AppKit overlay; Windows and Linux (X11 or XWayland) use PyAutoGUI. A Flutter client (macOS, Windows, Linux, web) talks to a local backend; the CLI runs tasks directly. The README headline of 75.0% on OSWorld is a model-provider figure. The repository was formerly iliyaZelenko/os-ai-computer-use (same GitHub repository ID).

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-777genius-os-ai-computer-use/record.json

## pebkac

自托管网页协同浏览工具，由运行本地 llama.cpp 模型的 smolagents 代码智能体编写 Python，通过 Zendriver 驱动 Chrome 浏览、按 CSS 选择器点击和输入、用 Trafilatura 提取文本及管理标签页。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-michaelsoftmd-pebkac-chrome/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/michaelsoftmd/pebkac-chrome
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、记忆、效率
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T16:09:40Z
- ID：github:michaelsoftmd/pebkac-chrome

### 范围记录

Containerized stack (Podman or Docker Compose): a FastAPI service wraps a Zendriver-controlled Chrome, and a SafeCodeAgent subclass of the smolagents CodeAgent calls it through tools for navigation, CSS-selector clicks, typing, key presses, Trafilatura content extraction, DuckDuckGo search, background tabs, API-response capture and a Cloudflare challenge handler, within a configurable step limit (default 10). The model reads page text and selectors; the screenshot tool only saves files, and the README lists vision assist as under consideration. Redis and DuckDB cache extracted content, per-site working selectors persist, and runs are logged to SQLite. The user watches and shares the browser over noVNC from a localhost control panel. The README markets anti-bot evasion and says the project is on hold. Root LICENSE is AGPL-3.0.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-michaelsoftmd-pebkac-chrome/record.json

## PokeClaw

Android 应用在设备端运行手机控制智能体，通过 Accessibility 服务读取 UI 树，并用设备端 Gemma 4 或可选云端模型执行点击、滑动和输入。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-agents-io-pokeclaw/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/agents-io/PokeClaw
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：Android
- 主题：手机操作、自动化
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T11:54:35Z
- ID：github:agents-io/pokeclaw

### 范围记录

Also called PocketClaw. The task loop runs inside the installed APK; ADB control from a host is outside this architecture. Local mode runs Gemma 4 through LiteRT-LM after a model download of about 2.6 GB; Cloud mode calls a hosted model. The tool registry also has notification, clipboard, call, file and knowledge-base tools, plus a separate D-pad tool set for Android TV devices. The README reports pass rates, for example 18 of 20 cloud tasks on a Pixel 8 Pro, from the author's trials.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-agents-io-pokeclaw/record.json

## qz-browser-agent

Chrome 扩展智能体读取页面 DOM 和无障碍树，跨标签页操作带索引的元素或截图坐标，并通过内置 CLI 和 MCP 桥接接收委派任务。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-alibaba-qz-browser-agent/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/alibaba/qz-browser-agent
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、自动化、工具集成
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T16:02:16Z
- ID：github:alibaba/qz-browser-agent

### 范围记录

Published under the alibaba GitHub organization as a Manifest V3 Chrome extension (Chrome Web Store, and a v0.1.2 extension ZIP as the latest GitHub release at review time). Page tools read the DOM (read_dom, find_elements) and act on indexed element targets (act_on_element); get_ax_tree takes a compact accessibility-tree snapshot through the Chrome debugger; the screenshot tool captures the tab through Chrome DevTools Protocol with a captureVisibleTab fallback, and act_on_screen clicks, drags, scrolls or types at screenshot coordinates. services/mcp-bridge is the npm package qz-browser-agent, a local CLI and MCP service that passes tasks to the paired extension. Models come from providers the user configures; docs/benchmarks.md measures side-panel rendering, a separate measure from task success.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-alibaba-qz-browser-agent/record.json

## Scout

TypeScript 浏览器智能体通过 CDP 操作任意 Chromium，按观察、行动、检查循环运行，可分别选择操作、推理、视觉和提取模型，并可输出符合 JSON Schema 的答案。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-outcrawlai-scout/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/outcrawlai/scout
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、规划
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-19T06:16:13Z
- ID：github:outcrawlai/scout

### 范围记录

Published by Outcrawl; the GitHub description calls the repository an automatic mirror of Outcrawl's production repository. Packages: scout (entry point), runtime (agent loop), model (prompts and provider-neutral clients), scout-core, scout-cdp (DevTools-protocol driver) and agenthands (local pointer and keyboard planning). It runs against a local browser, any CDP endpoint or Outcrawl's hosted browser, which needs an Outcrawl key and adds residential connections, captcha solving and replays. The README reports 92.5% page reach on Browser Use's 80-site benchmark. Root code is Apache-2.0; packages/agenthands keeps its own MIT license.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-outcrawlai-scout/record.json

## Surfer-H-CLI

已弃用的 H Company Surfer-H 网页智能体运行器：策略模型读取近期 Chrome 截图，Holo 定位器给出点击坐标，Selenium 执行操作，可选验证器检查答案。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-hcompai-surfer-h-cli/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/hcompai/surfer-h-cli
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、GUI 定位、界面理解
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T12:33:37Z
- ID：github:hcompai/surfer-h-cli

### 范围记录

Archived on GitHub, and the README marks it deprecated in favour of H Company's hosted Models API and Agent API and the hai-agents clients. The loop keeps the last three screenshots by default for the navigation step, which chooses click_element, write_element, scroll, go_back, refresh, wait, restart or answer; click and write targets are named elements that a localizer model (Holo1 or the structured Holo1.5 path) turns into viewport coordinates for Selenium ActionChains. Models are reached through OpenAI-compatible endpoints: H Company's hosted API, a local vLLM server, or a SageMaker deployment; one script uses GPT-4.1 as validator. A FastAPI agent_server.py and a Next.js frontend stream runs to a browser UI. Root LICENSE is the BSD 3-Clause text with an H Company copyright, covering the repository code.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-hcompai-surfer-h-cli/record.json

## TipTour

macOS 菜单栏智能体结合本地 Core ML 控件检测器与辅助功能树定位点击，由 TypeSafe 的 JEV 文本循环或 Gemini 实时语音驱动，并提供供其他智能体使用的本地主机接口。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-milind-soni-tiptour-macos/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/milind-soni/tiptour-macos
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、macOS
- 主题：GUI 定位、界面理解、人机协作
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-19T06:14:20Z
- ID：github:milind-soni/tiptour-macos

### 范围记录

JEV mode sends the typed task, locally detected labels and positions, and action history to TypeSafe's hosted jev-latest model (api.typesafe.ai); it performs only clicks and stops after 12 actions. Gemini mode receives microphone audio and, when enabled, screenshots. Video tours are built by the Elorian service and only point at controls. The localhost harness on 127.0.0.1:19474 exposes observe, ground-target and act endpoints. Requires macOS 14.2+ and an Xcode build; the repository has no releases. The MIT LICENSE also credits Farza's Clicky, the upstream fork source; bundled Neko sprites carry a BSD-2-Clause file.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-milind-soni-tiptour-macos/record.json

## typesafe-computer-use

计算机使用循环通过 OCR 和辅助功能标签读取屏幕，由托管的 TypeSafe 分类器以置信度选择下一步操作，仅在生成自由文本时调用写作模型。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-awlevin-typesafe-computer-use/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/awlevin/typesafe-computer-use
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、macOS、Windows、Linux、网页
- 主题：效率、动作预测、界面理解
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T11:53:03Z
- ID：github:awlevin/typesafe-computer-use

### 范围记录

Also called jev. The main loop drives macOS through Vision OCR, the accessibility tree and synthetic input; the Windows adapter (UI Automation, SendInput, Windows OCR) is documented as experimental and untested on Windows. An opt-in browser backend reads the DOM over the Chrome DevTools Protocol, and the Linux sandbox runs only that backend. Linux desktop control is through the OSWorld JevAgent adapter inside OSWorld's Ubuntu VM. Every decision calls the hosted TypeSafe API, so a TypeSafe key is required; an Anthropic or OpenAI-compatible writer is optional. Per-step cost and latency comparisons and the OSWorld run logs are the author's. The README marks the project beta.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-awlevin-typesafe-computer-use/record.json

## Wanta

面向工作智能体的 Electron 桌面宿主，内置 OpenCode 智能体及 Claude Code、Codex、Grok 适配器；集成浏览器让智能体和用户通过 ARIA 快照引用操作同一可见页面。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-oomol-lab-wanta/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/oomol-lab/wanta
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：相关研究
- 运行环境：网页
- 主题：网页导航、人机协作、工具集成
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T16:13:48Z
- ID：github:oomol-lab/wanta

### 范围记录

Recorded as adjacent: Wanta is a general agent host whose local tools are files, shell, skills, knowledge and SaaS connectors (OpenConnector), and its only graphical executor is the integrated browser. The Electron main process owns a WebContentsView per task page, Playwright attaches through a custom CDP transport over webContents.debugger, and an authenticated loopback bridge serves seven OpenCode tools: browser_navigate, browser_read (title, URL, dialog state and a Playwright AI-mode ARIA snapshot with refs), browser_click and browser_type on refs or unique selectors, browser_scroll, browser_screenshot and browser_dialog. The user shares the page and completes logins and CAPTCHAs; in Default Access the agent is told to hand sensitive actions to the user, which the design document calls a behavioural policy rather than a system guarantee. External agents keep their own accounts; hosted models, connectors and team workspaces need an OOMOL account. Packages for macOS, Windows and Linux. Root LICENSE is Apache-2.0.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-oomol-lab-wanta/record.json

## WebDroid Agent

在浏览器中运行的 Android 手机智能体，经 WebUSB/WebADB 连接设备，将截图及 uiautomator 文本树发送给兼容 OpenAI 或 Gemini 的视觉模型，再通过 ADB 执行其 JSON 操作。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-yeahhe365-webdroid-agent/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/yeahhe365/WebDroid-Agent
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：Android
- 主题：手机操作、人机协作、自动化
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-18T15:43:22Z
- ID：github:yeahhe365/webdroid-agent

### 范围记录

A React app that runs entirely in a Chromium browser (WebUSB through Tango/WebADB), or in Docker with a local proxy for model requests; the browser is the controller and the operated surface is a USB-connected Android device. Each step captures screencap output and, best effort, a compressed uiautomator dump of visible text, IDs, clickable state and bounds (src/lib/contextBuilder.ts); actions use pixel or 0-1000 normalized coordinates and become adb input, am start and keyboard-broadcast commands (src/adapters/inputCommands.ts). Sensitive taps can require confirmation, take_over hands control back to the user and type_secret sends the model only a secret ID; an unrestricted mode skips these checks. README.md is in Chinese and README.en-US.md is the English version. The repository was renamed from yeahhe365/webadb-autoglm (same repository ID 1244747615).

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-yeahhe365-webdroid-agent/record.json

## Xiryn

可自行托管的个人智能体应用，为每个智能体提供独立的 Docker Linux 桌面和 Chromium，通过截图及 xdotool 点击和按键操作，并支持实时接管、记忆、保险库和 Telegram 控制。

- 目录页面：https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-souravberaakaspeed-xiryn/
- 原始来源：https://github.com/SouravBeraAkaSpeed/xiryn
- 类型：智能体
- 审核状态：已审核
- 范围：核心
- 运行环境：桌面、Linux、网页
- 主题：自动化、网页导航、人机协作、记忆、工具集成
- 证据核查日期：2026-10-20T08:14:45Z
- ID：github:souravberaakaspeed/xiryn

### 范围记录

Next.js app run locally (version 0.4.1 in package.json; tag v0.4.1 points at b8016b5, and the inspected commit 489e9c4 is later). Each agent's workstation is a Docker container built from workstation/: Debian (trixie-slim) with Xvfb, xfwm4 and a panel, Chromium, x11vnc and noVNC, xdotool and scrot, a persistent home volume, and an inactivity watchdog. lib/agent/tools.ts defines the tool belt for the Vercel AI SDK streamText loop: shell and fs_* through docker exec; screen_capture and screen_share (scrot JPEG through xiryn-shot); pointer_click, pointer_move, pointer_drag, pointer_scroll, key_type and key_press (xdotool in lib/workstation.ts), each returning a fresh screenshot; request_takeover; vault_list, vault_fill, request_payment_approval and request_secret; memory_write and memory_forget; web_search and web_fetch through Firecrawl; and delegate for squads. Observation is plain screenshots of a 1280x800 display with pixel coordinates; there is no accessibility tree or element annotation. Before each step the runtime keeps only the two most recent screenshots in full (docs/architecture.md). The vault fills saved logins and cards into fields without the model seeing them, and cards only after the owner approves the payment. The user can watch and take over the desktop, and the agent hands over at logins, payments, one-time codes or CAPTCHAs, with a Telegram notice when paired. Models come from the owner's keys for OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, xAI, Groq, Mistral, DeepSeek, OpenRouter, Ollama, LM Studio or any OpenAI-compatible endpoint. The README's comparison with hosted agents is the author's own. Root LICENSE is MIT.

- 完整记录 JSON：https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-souravberaakaspeed-xiryn/record.json

## Yunkai

Android 手机助手，将模型驱动的工具调用与无障碍树读取、截图及点击、滑动、文本输入等基本操作结合；文档中的交互流程会向用户展示操作计划以供确认。

### 范围记录

The Android AccessibilityService contains concrete screen-reading and action paths. The inspected HarmonyOS accessibility extension is a lifecycle/event scaffold with its screen-content probe disabled; HDC appears in an external UI-test driver. Setup requires a configured OpenAI-compatible model endpoint and API key; local storage and the endpoint have separate data paths, with offline inference and transmission behavior unspecified. No app, device test or model inference was run.
